❌TT02Type 1 dealing range 25% + FVG (TikTok)4 שעותDIS—0 · —R—

שורה תחתונה

#אסטרטגיהגרףעסקאות בשנה האחרונהממוצע R חשבון $100K מ־30.04.2025 (סיכון 1%)שרד ורווחיעובר אתגר 2-StepTrading Prophet (הפסד צף דקה־דקה)סיכון 0.75%: חשבון + Prophet

הכללים שנבדקו

  • TV01 Gold Pro Strategy (zackseal): TV 'Gold Pro Strategy' (zackseal): EMA50 vs EMA200 trend + price on that side of EMA200, MACD(12,26,9) crossover, RSI(14) 50-70 for longs / 30-50 for shorts. Stop 3 ATR(14), trailing. No fixed target.
  • TV02 AsliGold DCH10 breakout: TV 'AsliGold XAUUSD DCH10 Trend Breakout': close crosses above the previous 10-bar high, ADX(14) > 20, long only. ATR(14) stop and target (multipliers not published: 2 / 4 assumed), time stop (20 bars assumed).
  • TV03 Golden Master v1 (EMA200 + MACD): TV 'Golden Master Strategy v1': close above EMA200, MACD(12,26,9) crosses above signal below zero, histogram rising. Stop = lowest low of the last 4 bars, target 1:3, break-even at 50% of the target. Long only.
  • TV04 Golden Edge Pro (MACD hist + volume): TV 'Golden Edge Pro': MACD(16,26,9) histogram crosses above 0, EMA6(vol) > EMA8(vol), volume > 50% of the previous bar. Target ATR(14) x 2 (not published, assumed); stop not published (1.5 ATR assumed); break-even at 69% of target -> entry + 0.48%. Long only.
  • TV05 control Scalper (UT Bot + ADX/RSI): TV 'control Scalper': UT Bot signals (key 1, ATR 10 - common defaults, not published), only when ADX(14) is between 16 and 20, RSI(14) > 55 for longs / < 45 for shorts. Exit on the opposite signal; the UT trailing line is the stop.
  • TV06 Supertrend 10/3: TV classic 'Supertrend Strategy' (ATR 10, factor 3): long on an up flip, short on a down flip, always in the market (exit = opposite flip); stop = 3 ATR as the risk unit.
  • TV07 London breakout (Asian range): London breakout (classic forex): the Asian range is 00:00-07:00 UTC. From 07:00 to 10:00 UTC, a close above the range high buys / below the low sells (first break only). Stop at the other side of the range, target 1.5R, everything closed at 16:00 UTC.
  • TV08 Bollinger + RSI mean reversion: Bollinger mean reversion: close below the lower band (20, 2) with RSI(14) < 30 buys, above the upper band with RSI > 70 sells; exit when price returns to the middle band. Stop 2 ATR(14).
  • TV09 Connors RSI(2): Connors RSI(2): above SMA200 buy when RSI(2) < 10, exit when close > SMA5; below SMA200 sell when RSI(2) > 90, exit when close < SMA5. Stop 3 ATR(14) (Connors uses none).
  • TV10 ICT Fair Value Gap: ICT Fair Value Gap: a bullish gap = low of bar i above high of bar i-2 after a strong bar. Within the next 20 bars, when price dips into the gap and the bar closes back above its bottom, buy at the next open. Stop just below the gap, target 2R. Mirror for sells.
  • TV11 Liquidity sweep of previous day: Liquidity sweep reversal (SMC): price trades beyond the previous day's high/low and the bar closes back inside -> fade it. Stop beyond the sweep's extreme, target 2R.
  • TV12 Daily pivot bounce S1/R1: Daily floor pivots: P = (H+L+C)/3 of the previous day, S1 = 2P-H, R1 = 2P-L. Price dips below S1 and closes back above -> buy, target P; mirror at R1. Stop half the S1-P distance beyond S1 (R1).
  • TT01 First 4H candle false breakout (TikTok): TT01 First 4H candle false breakout (@tradingdata2). New York time. Range = high/low of the day's first 4H candle (assumed 00:00-04:00 NY), usable once it has closed and until the NY day ends. On the 5-minute chart: a candle BODY closes outside the range, then a candle closes back inside -> fade it (broke up = short, broke down = long). Stop at the extreme of the breakout move, target 2R. Several trades per day allowed. Not modelled: moving the stop to a 'nearer key level' when the breakout is very wide.
  • TT02 Type 1 dealing range 25% + FVG (TikTok): TT02 Type 1 dealing range (@ali_khanict). False breakout below a 20-bar consolidation (close back inside within 3 bars), then expansion above the consolidation high. Dealing range = false-break low to expansion high. Buy when price retraces to the 25% level and that level sits inside a bullish fair value gap of the expansion. Targets in the video: 50% level and range high -> modelled as target = range high with the stop moved to break-even at the 50% level. Stop below the range low (not stated; assumed). Mirror for shorts (assumed).
  • TT03 ICT sweep+POI+IFVG+CISD (TikTok): TT03 Sweep + HTF POI + IFVG + CISD (@alawadtrd). All four, in order (short shown, long mirrored): (1) price sweeps a major high (above the 20-bar high); (2) the sweep trades into a bearish 15-minute FVG (higher-timeframe point of interest); (3) a bullish FVG on the entry chart is then closed through (it inverts = IFVG); (4) CISD: a close below the open of the latest run of up-candles. Enter on the CISD close, stop above the IFVG top, target 2R (stated 1:2). Window: 40 bars after the sweep (assumed).
  • TT04 Cup and handle breakout (TikTok): TT04 Cup and handle (@smart.forex.pips). Edge = a swing high (5 bars each side). Price falls at least 2 ATR, bottoms, and comes back to within 0.25 x depth of the edge within 20-150 bars (the cup; bottom in the middle 60% of the cup = 'rounded', assumed). Then a pullback under the edge no deeper than half the cup (the handle). Entry: a candle closes above the edge. Stop under the handle low. Target = edge + cup depth. Long only (as in the video).
  • TT11 NY 9:30 opening range breakout 15m, 2.5R (TikTok): TT11 NY opening-range breakout, 15 minutes (@_itskinglouis, NAS100). Mark the high/low of the 9:30 New York 15-minute candle. First full candle close above (below) the range -> enter at the next candle. Stop below (above) the recent low (high) = extreme of the last 3 candles (assumed). Target 2.5R. One trade per day; only until 16:00 NY (assumed).
  • TT12 Fibonacci golden zone pullback 5m (TikTok): TT12 Fibonacci golden zone pullback, 5 minutes (@il.gufo.notturno). Trend up when price breaks the recent swing high (20-bar high). Fib from the swing low before the break to the new high. When price pulls back into 0.618-0.79 and a bullish candle closes there, buy. Stop just below the 0.79 level (stated), target 1:2 (stated). Mirror for downtrends.
  • TT13 Opening range break and retest 5m (TikTok): TT13 Opening range breakout and retest, 5 minutes (@thecrypteller, also @cyforex2). Range = first 5-minute candle after the 9:30 NY open. Wait for a close beyond the range, then the first candle that taps the broken level and closes back on the breakout side -> enter in the breakout direction. Stop beyond the other side of the range. Target 2R. One trade per day.
  • TT16 Previous NY session high/low fade, 4R (TikTok): TT16 Previous New York session high/low fade (@forextristan, US30). Mark yesterday's NY session (9:30-16:00 NY) high and low. Today, during the NY session, the first touch of either level is faded: short at the high, long at the low. Stop slightly beyond the level (0.1 ATR, assumed). Target 4R (stated).
  • TT19 15m candle range sweep on 1m (TikTok): TT19 15-minute candle range sweep, executed on 1 minute (@protradingschool0). Range = the previous completed 15-minute candle. On the 1-minute chart, price breaks the range and a candle closes back inside -> enter at that close, against the break. Stop beyond the sweep's extreme. Target: 'next key level' in the video -> 2R assumed.
  • TT35 London 1H opening candle sweep + FVG (TikTok): TT35 London opening 1-hour candle range (@wesleytfxc, @primeswingtrader). Range = the 08:00-09:00 London candle. A wick through one side (the body back inside) -> on the lower timeframe enter at the first fair value gap retest in the reversal direction. Stop just beyond the FVG, target the opposite end of the 1-hour candle. Window until 12:00 London (assumed).
  • TT36 London Twilight Model 2AM range (TikTok): TT36 'London Twilight Model' (@massimotsr, 5 videos). Range = the 2:00 AM New York 1-hour candle. Sweep of its high (low), then on 1 minute an inverted FVG / order block in the reversal direction -> enter. Stop at the swing extreme of the sweep, target the opposite end of the 2 AM range. Window 03:00-08:00 NY (assumed). The optional higher-timeframe key level tap is not modelled.
  • TT39 ICT Silver Bullet 10-11 NY (TikTok): TT39 ICT Silver Bullet (@blosefx, @fluxchart, @the.trading.geek1). Only 10:00-11:00 NY. A sweep of the prior 60-bar high (low), then an FVG in the reversal direction forms and is retested -> enter. Stop beyond the sweep extreme (@solidtrader85's correction), target 2R (stated minimum).
  • TT40 Opening range + VWAP filter, 1:3 (TikTok): TT40 Opening range + VWAP (@horiizonfx, gold). Range 15:00-15:30 Paris = 09:00-09:30 NY. A close beyond it counts only if the session VWAP (from NY midnight) is on the other side of price (break down -> VWAP above). Wait for a retest of the broken edge that closes back outside -> enter. Stop just beyond the VWAP, target 1:3. One trade per day.
  • TT41 NY 8AM 15m candle break and retest, 1:3 (TikTok): TT41 New York 8:00 AM 15-minute candle break and retest (@rp_profits). Zone = the 08:00 ET 15-minute candle. From 09:00 ET the first close beyond the zone sets the direction; the retest of the zone that closes back on the breakout side is the entry. Stop slightly beyond the other side of the zone, target 1:3 (stated). One trade per day, until 12:00 ET.
  • TT45 EMA 9/21 crossover 1H, 1:2 (TikTok): TT45 EMA 9/21 crossover on 1 hour (@whitehattraders27). Bullish cross buys, bearish cross sells. Stop below/above the recent swing (lowest/highest of 10 bars, assumed). Target 1:2.
  • TT56 Break and retest of swing level, 2:1 (TikTok): TT56 Break and retest of a swing level (@user1239509627, @tarzantrader, @realnolanivey, @wchampfx, @savvybtrades). Level = the 20-bar high (low). A close through it, then within 10 bars a retest that touches the level and closes back on the breakout side -> enter. Stop beyond the retested level (0.3 ATR, assumed), target 2:1 (stated by @realnolanivey).
  • YT01 Opening range false-breakout fade, 2R (YouTube): YT01 Opening-range false-breakout fade (YouTube 5a8mNmb9VzM, NQ/ES). Range = the first bar after 9:30 NY (the video uses a 30-second candle; 1-minute substitute here). Price trades beyond the range and a candle closes back inside -> enter against the breakout at that close. Stop at the extreme of the failed breakout, target 2R, one trade per day, flat at 11:00 NY (assumed). The video's break-even after one hour is not modelled.
  • YT03 Supply/demand zone first retest (YouTube): YT03 Supply/demand zone, first retest (YouTube LpeThhwHiGA). Impulse = 3 same-direction candles moving > 1.5 ATR, or one candle with body > 1.5 ATR. Zone = the last opposite-colour candle before the impulse. First return to the zone within 100 bars -> enter. Stop beyond the zone wick, target the swing extreme from which the return began. Skip when reward/risk < 1.5 (assumed thresholds as listed).
  • TV13 Turtle System 1 (20/10 breakout): Turtle System 1 (Dennis/Faith): close above the 20-bar high buys, below the 20-bar low sells. Stop 2N (N = ATR20). Exit on the opposite 10-bar breakout. No target.
  • TV14 Turtle System 2 (55/20 breakout): Turtle System 2: 55-bar breakout entries, stop 2N, exit on the opposite 20-bar breakout.
  • TV15 Keltner channel breakout + ATR trail: Keltner channel breakout (Chester Keltner / Linda Raschke version): close above EMA20 + 2 x ATR10 buys, below EMA20 - 2 x ATR10 sells. Stop 2 ATR, ATR trailing stop 3 ATR.
  • TV16 Golden cross 50/200 + ATR trail: 50/200 moving-average trend (golden/death cross): SMA50 crossing above SMA200 buys, below sells. Stop 3 ATR, ATR trailing stop 3 ATR, reverse on the opposite cross.
  • TV17 Bollinger squeeze breakout: Bollinger squeeze breakout (John Bollinger): band width (20, 2) at its lowest of the last 120 bars marks a squeeze; within the next 10 bars a close outside the band enters in that direction. Stop 2 ATR, ATR trailing stop 2.5 ATR.
  • TV18 Inside-bar breakout, 2R: Inside-bar breakout: bar i-1 is inside bar i-2 (the mother bar); a close above the mother high buys, below the mother low sells. Stop at the other side of the mother bar, target 2R.
  • TV19 RSI trend pullback (EMA50/200), 3R: Trend pullback with RSI (Andrew Cardwell style): uptrend when EMA50 > EMA200; RSI(14) dips under 40 and crosses back above 40 -> buy (mirror at 60 in downtrends). Stop 2 ATR, target 3R.
  • TV20 Ichimoku TK cross above/below cloud: Ichimoku (Hosoda, 9/26/52): Tenkan crosses above Kijun while price is above the cloud -> buy (mirror below). Stop at Kijun (assumed via level), ATR trail 3, exit when price closes back inside/through the cloud.
  • TV21 Parabolic SAR + EMA200: Parabolic SAR (Wilder, 0.02/0.2) with EMA200 filter: SAR flips below price while price is above EMA200 -> buy (mirror). Stop trails on the SAR line.
  • TV22 MACD zero-line cross + EMA200, 2R: MACD (12/26/9) zero-line trend: MACD line crosses above zero with price above EMA200 -> buy (mirror). Stop 2 ATR, target 2R.

  • TV26 NR7 breakout, 2R: NR7 breakout (Toby Crabel): the bar with the narrowest range of the last 7; a close above its high on the next bar buys, below its low sells. Stop at the other side of the NR7 bar, target 2R.
  • TV27 Previous-day high/low breakout, 2R: Previous-day high/low breakout: first close above yesterday's high buys, below yesterday's low sells (once per day each). Stop at yesterday's midpoint, target 2R.
  • TV28 Stochastic pullback in trend, 2R: Stochastic pullback in trend (Lane, 14/3/3): uptrend (close > EMA200), %K crosses above %D below 20 -> buy (mirror above 80 in downtrend). Stop 1.5 ATR, target 2R.
  • TV29 Z-score mean reversion: Z-score mean reversion: z = (close - SMA20) / stdev20. z < -2 buys, z > 2 sells; exit back at the mean (z crosses 0). Stop 2.5 ATR.
  • TV30 IBS mean reversion (long only): Internal Bar Strength (index mean reversion): IBS = (close - low) / (high - low). IBS < 0.2 with close above SMA200 buys; exit when IBS > 0.8 or at a 2.5 ATR stop. Long only.
  • TV31 Engulfing at 20-bar extreme, 2R: Engulfing at a swing extreme: bullish engulfing whose low is the lowest of 20 bars -> buy (mirror at a 20-bar high). Stop beyond the pattern, target 2R.
  • TV32 Previous-week high/low breakout, 2R: Previous-week high/low breakout: first close above last week's high buys, below last week's low sells (once per week each). Stop at last week's midpoint, target 2R.
  • TV33 Keltner fade to the mean: Keltner fade: a close back inside EMA20 +/- 2.5 ATR after closing outside -> fade toward the EMA (exit at the EMA). Stop 1.5 ATR.

  • TV36 EMA20 bounce in strong trend, 2R: EMA20 bounce in a strong trend: EMA20 > EMA50 and both rising, the bar touches EMA20 and closes above it as a bullish candle -> buy (mirror). Stop below the bar low - 0.5 ATR, 2R.
  • TV37 Three-bar reversal at 20-bar extreme, 2R: Three-bar reversal at a 20-bar extreme: bar i-1 makes the lowest low of 20 bars and bar i closes above the high of bar i-1 -> buy (mirror). Stop below bar i-1's low, target 2R.
  • TV38 Donchian 10 fade + RSI(2) (Connors): Donchian extreme fade (Connors style): close at a new 10-bar low with RSI(2) < 5 and close above SMA200 -> buy; mirror. Exit when close crosses the 5-bar SMA. Stop 2.5 ATR.
  • TV39 Chandelier exit trend (LeBeau): Chandelier exit trend (Chuck LeBeau): long line = 22-bar highest high - 3 ATR22, short line = 22-bar lowest low + 3 ATR22. Close crossing above the short line buys (trend flips up), below the long line sells. Stop trails on the chandelier line.

נכסים בלי נתונים: אין
